**Analysis:**
The bug report claims that the minimize animation is "less smooth" when triggered by the Back button compared to the in-player gesture, which is described as "perfectly fluid."

My findings confirm the core of the issue:
1.  **Lag is Present:** All captured animations have very low frame rates (between 3.6 and 4.2 fps), which is far from "perfectly fluid" and can be described as laggy or stuttering.
2.  **Back Button is Worst in Audio Mode:** The lowest frame rate observed was 3.6 fps, which occurred when using the Back button in Audio mode. This confirms that the animation is particularly laggy under these specific conditions.
3.  **Discrepancy in Video Mode:** In video mode, the Back button animation (~4.2 fps) was marginally *faster* than the swipe gesture animation (~4.0 fps). This contradicts the bug report's specific comparison for video mode, but both are still very laggy.

The critical evidence is the objectively poor performance (3.6 fps) when using the Back button in audio mode, which validates the user's primary complaint of a "laggy animation." Although the "perfectly fluid" description for the gesture might be an exaggeration, the performance difference, especially in audio mode, is measurable and confirms the buggy behavior.
